SEPTEMBER 2026 CURRENT AFFAIRS — IBPS MAINS MCQ QUESTION BANK
Format: plain text; one record per question; backend-ingestible fields.
Current question count: 172
NOTE: This is an interim bank and does NOT yet meet the requested minimum of 80 questions per PDF.
Questions are grouped by source PDF, then topic. Each question includes options, answer, explanation, difficulty, and memory tip.

TOPIC: National Affairs
------------------------------------------------------------
ID: SEP22-NAT-001
Month: September 2026
Topic: National Affairs
Subtopic: Defence exercise
Difficulty: Easy
Source: Toppers-CA-September-22-2026-by-AffairsCloud-1-v01.pdf
Question: Which two countries began the 13th SLINEX-26 maritime exercise?
A. India and Sri Lanka
B. India and Bangladesh
C. India and Thailand
D. India and Indonesia
Correct answer: A
Explanation: The PDF reports that India and Sri Lanka began the 13th edition of SLINEX-26.
Memory tip: SLINEX = Sri Lanka–India naval exercise.
